headline: National Advertising Division Finds Carpe’s Reference Price Supported; Recommends Carpe Modify or Discontinue Savings and Comparative Claims
<<<SUMMARY>>>
The National Advertising Division ruled that Carpe's $19.95 reference price for its Underarm Stick deodorant is reasonable, but recommended the company modify or discontinue multiple advertising claims including savings offers, free shipping, comparative performance claims, and its '100-hour sweat and odor control' claim. The NAD found Carpe failed to clearly disclose subscription requirements for savings and lacked support for comparative claims against competitors.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
PG | positive | P&G successfully challenged Carpe's advertising claims, resulting in NAD recommendations against Carpe's comparative claims that implied P&G's competing products are ineffective. This outcome protects P&G's market position and validates concerns about competitor misleading advertising.

<<<INSIGHTS>>>
